---------- ModelClientStreamingChunkEvent (summarizer_agent) ----------
Here is the summary of the requirements and scope for the AI dialer project:

---

### **Product Overview**
- **Description**: An AI dialer system to call customers offering travel packages. If a customer accepts, the call transfers to a human agent; if declined, the AI attempts up to three additional persuading attempts before ending the call.
- **Target Users**: Travel agencies, airlines, and their targeted customers.

---

### **Key Features and Functionalities**
#### **Scope Included:**
1. **Call System Integration**:
   - Make and transfer calls to human agents via a call center system.
2. **CRM Integration**:
   - Fetch customer details (name, phone, location, salary).
3. **Database Connectivity**:
   - Access predefined offers and save call results:
     - Successful call
     - Closed deals
     - Rejected deals
     - Call errors
   - Store predefined answers and RAG info to respond to customer questions.
4. **Metrics and Reporting**:
   - Generate end-of-shift reports with KPIs and metrics.
   - Configurable KPIs include: successful calls, error calls, successful deals, rejected deals.
   - Reports with charts, tables, and a dynamic dashboard.

#### **Scope Excluded (Future Phases):**
1. WhatsApp and social media integrations.

---

### **Non-Functional Requirements**
1. **Performance**:
   - Support up to 300 simultaneous calls.
2. **Availability and Reliability**:
   - 99.96% availability.
   - Real-time response processing.
3. **Language & Voice Quality**:
   - English-only.
   - Use natural language with human-like voice quality.
   - Tone is respectful but persuasive.
4. **Security**:
   - Encrypted data to comply with GDPR and PII regulations.
   - Role-based access:
     - Full access for developers.
     - Reporting access for marketing teams.
5. **Usability**:
   - Follow best UI/UX design practices.

---

### **Systems and Integrations**
- **Mandatory Integrations**:
  - Call center systems for making and transferring calls.
  - CRM for accessing customer data.
  - Databases for predefined offers, call results, answers, and RAG information.
- **Dependency Management**:
  - Two cloud providers and two SIP trunk providers for redundancy.

---

### **Constraints and Assumptions**
1. Compliance:
   - Must adhere to regulatory requirements such as GDPR and PII.
2. Offers and customer responses:
   - Predefined and updatable by the marketing team as required.
3. Hours of Operation:
   - Calls are restricted to 8:00 AM to 5:00 PM.

---

### **User Personas and Roles**
1. **Internal Users**:
   - **Marketing Teams**:
     - Manage predefined offers, responses, and documentation.
   - **Human Agents**:
     - Receive transferred calls to handle and close deals.
   - **IT Teams**:
     - Provide technical support and development adjustments.
   - **Project Sponsor**:
     - Marketing VP oversees funding and priorities.
2. **External Users**:
   - Travel agencies, airlines, and final target customers.

---

### **Dependencies and Risks**
- **Dependencies**:
  - Reliance on cloud hosting providers and SIP trunk services.
- **Risk Mitigation**:
  - Ensure redundancy by using two cloud providers and two SIP trunk providers.

---

Would you like to add or clarify any information, or do you APPROVE the summary?
If it's final, please reply with **APPROVE SUMMARY**.

# Use Cases and User Stories for AI Dialer System

## Use Cases

### **Use Case 1: Make an Outbound Call**
#### Description:
The AI Dialer enables human agents to make outbound calls efficiently to customers, providing behavioral insights to tailor the call experience.

#### Actors:
- Human Agents
- Customers

#### Preconditions:
1. The agent must be logged into the AI Dialer system.
2. The customer’s contact information must exist in the database.
3. The outbound call feature must be enabled for the agent.

#### Postconditions:
1. The customer receives the call.
2. The system records call details such as duration, outcome, and notes.

#### Main Flow:
1. The agent initiates the outbound call through the AI Dialer system.
2. The AI Dialer retrieves the customer's contact information from the database.
3. The system analyzes the mood and behavior of the customer using past interactions or real-time data.
4. The dialer connects the call to the customer.
5. The agent communicates with the customer.
6. At the end of the call, the system prompts the agent to record notes and the call outcome.
7. If applicable, the system suggests follow-up actions based on the call's result.

#### Alternate Flows:
1. **Call Fails to Connect:**
   1.1 The system retries the call three times before marking it as "Failed to Connect."
   1.2 A notification is sent to the agent regarding failure.

2. **Customer Busy:**
   2.1 The system schedules a callback and notifies the agent.

---

### **Use Case 2: Generate Call Reports**
#### Description:
The AI Dialer provides detailed call reports for the marketing team and IT support to analyze performance and troubleshoot issues.

#### Actors:
- Marketing Teams
- IT Support

#### Preconditions:
1. Reports must have data from completed calls.
2. The user must have access permissions to view reports.

#### Postconditions:
1. A summarized report is generated with metrics like call duration, success rate, and agent performance.
2. Users can download or share the report for further analysis.

#### Main Flow:
1. The user logs into the AI Dialer system.
2. The user navigates to the reporting module.
3. The user requests a custom report by selecting filters such as date range, agent ID, or call outcomes.
4. The system processes and generates the report.
5. The user views the report on the screen and downloads it if necessary.

#### Alternate Flows:
1. **Missing Data:**
   1.1 The system alerts the user about incomplete or missing records relevant to the report.
   1.2 The user can adjust the filters or wait for the data to refresh.

2. **Export Error:**
   2.1 The system retries the export process automatically.
   2.2 If unsuccessful, the system prompts the user to make manual corrections.

---

### **Use Case 3: Update Customer Database**
#### Description:
The AI Dialer allows IT support to update customer information in the database to ensure accurate contact and interaction records.

#### Actors:
- IT Support

#### Preconditions:
1. The user (IT Support) must have administrative access to the database.
2. The system must have an operational database linked to the AI Dialer.

#### Postconditions:
1. Customer information is updated and validated in the database.
2. The system reflects updated data for calls.

#### Main Flow:
1. IT support logs into the AI Dialer system.
2. IT support navigates to the database management module.
3. IT support searches for the relevant customer record using filters (e.g., name, phone number).
4. IT support updates the customer details, such as phone number, email, or preferences.
5. The system validates the changes and synchronizes them across related modules.
6. IT support receives confirmation of a successful update.

#### Alternate Flows:
1. **Validation Errors:**
   1.1 The system flags validation errors during the update process (e.g., incomplete fields).
   1.2 IT support corrects the errors before resubmitting.

2. **Conflict with Existing Records:**
   2.1 If duplicate records are detected, the system recommends merging instead of updating.

---

### **Use Case 4: Behavioral AI Insights During Calls**
#### Description:
The AI Dialer provides real-time behavioral insights to human agents during calls.

#### Actors:
- Human Agents
- Customers

#### Preconditions:
1. The AI Dialer is configured to provide behavioral analysis.
2. The agent is actively on a call with the customer.

#### Postconditions:
1. The agent receives real-time analysis to improve communication.
2. The system logs the behavioral insights for future use.

#### Main Flow:
1. The AI Dialer analyzes customer behavior in real-time using voice tone, language patterns, and pauses.
2. The system displays insights (e.g., "Customer appears frustrated") to the agent during the call.
3. The agent adjusts their communication style accordingly.
4. Insights are saved in the customer profile for future calls.

---

## User Stories

### **Outbound Calls**
1. **Human Agent:**
   *As a human agent, I want to use the AI Dialer to make calls, so that I can quickly reach customers with accurate insights.*

   #### Acceptance Criteria:
   - The system must connect calls in under five seconds.
   - The system must retrieve and display customer information on-call initiation.

---

### **Reporting and Analysis**
1. **Marketing Team:**
   *As a marketing team member, I want to generate call reports, so that I can analyze campaign effectiveness.*

   #### Acceptance Criteria:
   - Reports must include metrics like call duration, success rate, and conversions.
   - Reports must be exportable in formats like CSV and PDF.

2. **IT Support:**
   *As IT support, I want to view error reports, so that I can troubleshoot call connectivity issues.*

   #### Acceptance Criteria:
   - Error reports must include timestamps and error codes.
   - Reports must highlight recurring issues with potential solutions.

---

### **Database Management**
1. **IT Support:**
   *As IT support, I want to update customer information, so that the database reflects accurate contact details.*

   #### Acceptance Criteria:
   - The system must prevent duplicate records.
   - Updates must synchronize across all modules.

---

### **Behavioral AI Insights**
1. **Human Agent:**
   *As a human agent, I want real-time behavioral AI insights during calls, so that I can improve my communication with customers.*

   #### Acceptance Criteria:
   - Behavioral insights must appear in the agent's dashboard during the call.
   - Insights must update dynamically based on new customer inputs.

---

### **Customer Perspective**
1. **Customer:**
   *As a customer, I want agents to understand my preferences during calls, so that my experience feels personalized.*

   #### Acceptance Criteria:
   - The system must store and retrieve customer preferences.
   - Notes and insights from previous calls must guide future interactions.

---

**READYid='4e64d661-cb7e-4bf5-a9a7-a71594703e0f' source='functional_spec_writer_agent' models_usage=None metadata={} created_at=datetime.datetime(2025, 8, 9, 20, 53, 47, 766543, tzinfo=datetime.timezone.utc) content=[FunctionExecutionResult(content='use_case_agent: # Use Cases and User Stories for AI Dialer System\n\n## Use Cases\n\n### **Use Case 1: Make an Outbound Call**\n#### Description:\nThe AI Dialer enables human agents to make outbound calls efficiently to customers, providing behavioral insights to tailor the call experience.\n\n#### Actors:\n- Human Agents  \n- Customers  \n\n#### Preconditions:\n1. The agent must be logged into the AI Dialer system.  \n2. The customer’s contact information must exist in the database.  \n3. The outbound call feature must be enabled for the agent.\n\n#### Postconditions:\n1. The customer receives the call.  \n2. The system records call details such as duration, outcome, and notes.\n\n#### Main Flow:\n1. The agent initiates the outbound call through the AI Dialer system.  \n2. The AI Dialer retrieves the customer\'s contact information from the database.  \n3. The system analyzes the mood and behavior of the customer using past interactions or real-time data.  \n4. The dialer connects the call to the customer.  \n5. The agent communicates with the customer.  \n6. At the end of the call, the system prompts the agent to record notes and the call outcome.  \n7. If applicable, the system suggests follow-up actions based on the call\'s result.  \n\n#### Alternate Flows:\n1. **Call Fails to Connect:**  \n   1.1 The system retries the call three times before marking it as "Failed to Connect."  \n   1.2 A notification is sent to the agent regarding failure.  \n \n2. **Customer Busy:**  \n   2.1 The system schedules a callback and notifies the agent.  \n\n---\n\n### **Use Case 2: Generate Call Reports**\n#### Description:\nThe AI Dialer provides detailed call reports for the marketing team and IT support to analyze performance and troubleshoot issues.\n\n#### Actors:\n- Marketing Teams  \n- IT Support  \n\n#### Preconditions:\n1. Reports must have data from completed calls.  \n2. The user must have access permissions to view reports.\n\n#### Postconditions:\n1. A summarized report is generated with metrics like call duration, success rate, and agent performance.  \n2. Users can download or share the report for further analysis.\n\n#### Main Flow:\n1. The user logs into the AI Dialer system.  \n2. The user navigates to the reporting module.  \n3. The user requests a custom report by selecting filters such as date range, agent ID, or call outcomes.  \n4. The system processes and generates the report.  \n5. The user views the report on the screen and downloads it if necessary.\n\n#### Alternate Flows:\n1. **Missing Data:**  \n   1.1 The system alerts the user about incomplete or missing records relevant to the report.  \n   1.2 The user can adjust the filters or wait for the data to refresh.  \n\n2. **Export Error:**  \n   2.1 The system retries the export process automatically.  \n   2.2 If unsuccessful, the system prompts the user to make manual corrections.  \n\n---\n\n### **Use Case 3: Update Customer Database**\n#### Description:\nThe AI Dialer allows IT support to update customer information in the database to ensure accurate contact and interaction records.\n\n#### Actors:\n- IT Support  \n\n#### Preconditions:\n1. The user (IT Support) must have administrative access to the database.  \n2. The system must have an operational database linked to the AI Dialer.\n\n#### Postconditions:\n1. Customer information is updated and validated in the database.  \n2. The system reflects updated data for calls.\n\n#### Main Flow:\n1. IT support logs into the AI Dialer system.  \n2. IT support navigates to the database management module.  \n3. IT support searches for the relevant customer record using filters (e.g., name, phone number).  \n4. IT support updates the customer details, such as phone number, email, or preferences.  \n5. The system validates the changes and synchronizes them across related modules.  \n6. IT support receives confirmation of a successful update.\n\n#### Alternate Flows:\n1. **Validation Errors:**  \n   1.1 The system flags validation errors during the update process (e.g., incomplete fields).  \n   1.2 IT support corrects the errors before resubmitting.  \n\n2. **Conflict with Existing Records:**  \n   2.1 If duplicate records are detected, the system recommends merging instead of updating.  \n\n---\n\n### **Use Case 4: Behavioral AI Insights During Calls**\n#### Description:\nThe AI Dialer provides real-time behavioral insights to human agents during calls.\n\n#### Actors:\n- Human Agents  \n- Customers  \n\n#### Preconditions:\n1. The AI Dialer is configured to provide behavioral analysis.  \n2. The agent is actively on a call with the customer.\n\n#### Postconditions:\n1. The agent receives real-time analysis to improve communication.  \n2. The system logs the behavioral insights for future use.\n\n#### Main Flow:\n1. The AI Dialer analyzes customer behavior in real-time using voice tone, language patterns, and pauses.  \n2. The system displays insights (e.g., "Customer appears frustrated") to the agent during the call.  \n3. The agent adjusts their communication style accordingly.  \n4. Insights are saved in the customer profile for future calls.  \n\n---\n\n## User Stories\n\n### **Outbound Calls**\n1. **Human Agent:**  \n   *As a human agent, I want to use the AI Dialer to make calls, so that I can quickly reach customers with accurate insights.*  \n\n   #### Acceptance Criteria:  \n   - The system must connect calls in under five seconds.  \n   - The system must retrieve and display customer information on-call initiation.  \n\n---\n\n### **Reporting and Analysis**\n1. **Marketing Team:**  \n   *As a marketing team member, I want to generate call reports, so that I can analyze campaign effectiveness.*  \n\n   #### Acceptance Criteria:  \n   - Reports must include metrics like call duration, success rate, and conversions.  \n   - Reports must be exportable in formats like CSV and PDF.  \n\n2. **IT Support:**  \n   *As IT support, I want to view error reports, so that I can troubleshoot call connectivity issues.*  \n\n   #### Acceptance Criteria:  \n   - Error reports must include timestamps and error codes.  \n   - Reports must highlight recurring issues with potential solutions.  \n\n---\n\n### **Database Management**\n1. **IT Support:**  \n   *As IT support, I want to update customer information, so that the database reflects accurate contact details.*  \n\n   #### Acceptance Criteria:  \n   - The system must prevent duplicate records.  \n   - Updates must synchronize across all modules.  \n\n---\n\n### **Behavioral AI Insights**\n1. **Human Agent:**  \n   *As a human agent, I want real-time behavioral AI insights during calls, so that I can improve my communication with customers.*  \n\n   #### Acceptance Criteria:  \n   - Behavioral insights must appear in the agent\'s dashboard during the call.  \n   - Insights must update dynamically based on new customer inputs.  \n\n---\n\n### **Customer Perspective**\n1. **Customer:**  \n   *As a customer, I want agents to understand my preferences during calls, so that my experience feels personalized.*  \n\n   #### Acceptance Criteria:  \n   - The system must store and retrieve customer preferences.  \n   - Notes and insights from previous calls must guide future interactions.  \n\n---\n\n**READY**', name='use_case_agent', call_id='call_Y3Xs7n5K6RYiFW7Cps49EhwI', is_error=False)] type='ToolCallExecutionEvent'

Error reports must include timestamps and error codes.  \n   - Reports must highlight recurring issues with potential solutions.  \n\n---\n\n### **Database Management**\n1. **IT Support:**  \n   *As IT support, I want to update customer information, so that the database reflects accurate contact details.*  \n\n   #### Acceptance Criteria:  \n   - The system must prevent duplicate records.  \n   - Updates must synchronize across all modules.  \n\n---\n\n### **Behavioral AI Insights**\n1. **Human Agent:**  \n   *As a human agent, I want real-time behavioral AI insights during calls, so that I can improve my communication with customers.*  \n\n   #### Acceptance Criteria:  \n   - Behavioral insights must appear in the agent\'s dashboard during the call.  \n   - Insights must update dynamically based on new customer inputs.  \n\n---\n\n### **Customer Perspective**\n1. **Customer:**  \n   *As a customer, I want agents to understand my preferences during calls, so that my experience feels personalized.*  \n\n   #### Acceptance Criteria:  \n   - The system must store and retrieve customer preferences.  \n   - Notes and insights from previous calls must guide future interactions.  \n\n---\n\n**READY**', name='use_case_agent', call_id='call_Y3Xs7n5K6RYiFW7Cps49EhwI', is_error=False)]

The AI will handle call interactions, transfer customers to human agents for finalizing deals, and aim to improve efficiency and customer engagement. By leveraging integrations with existing systems, the AI dialer will streamline outbound campaigns and provide comprehensive reporting on KPIs.\n\n---\n\n## Scope\n### In-Scope Features\n1. Integration with the call center to:\n   - Initiate and transfer calls.\n   - Support up to 300 simultaneous calls.\n2. Integration with CRM systems for accessing the following customer details:\n   - Name, phone number, location, and salary.\n3. Database usage for:\n   - Predefined offers and responses.\n   - Storing call results (successful, rejected, or error calls).\n   - Storing additional resources for generating responses to customer questions using a Rules and Guidance System (RAG).\n4. Generation of end-of-shift metrics and KPIs, which include:\n   - Successful calls, errors, successful deals, and rejected deals.\n   - Reports featuring charts, tables, and a dynamic dashboard.\n5. Real-time decision-making based on predefined offers and call status.\n\n### Out-of-Scope Features\n1. Integration with WhatsApp or other social networks.\n2. Expansion to non-English languages.\n\n---\n\n## Functional Requirements\n1. The AI dialer must integrate with the call center system to initiate, manage, and transfer calls in real time.\n2. The AI must access relevant customer data from the CRM system during the call execution process.\n3. The system must store and retrieve predefined offers and responses from a database.\n4. The AI should generate reports at the end of each shift, summarizing KPIs and metrics.\n5. The system should allow marketing teams to update offers and predefined responses in the database.\n6. Calls should operate only within the hours of 8:00 AM to 5:00 PM.\n7. All data, including call results and customer interactions, must be stored securely with encryption.\n\n---\n\n## Non-Functional Requirements\n1. **Performance**:\n   - Support up to 300 simultaneous calls.\n2. **Availability and Reliability**:\n   - Ensure a system availability of 99.96%.\n3. **Language & Voice Quality**:\n   - Support only the English language.\n   - Use natural language processing to achieve human-like voice quality with respectful and convincing tones.\n4. **Security**:\n   - Comply with GDPR and PII regulations.\n   - Implement access controls: developers have full access, while the marketing team has restricted access to reports.\n5. **Usability**:\n   - Follow best practices for UI/UX design.\n\n---\n\n## User Roles & Personas\n\n### Internal Users\n1. **Marketing Team**:\n   - Updates predefined offers, answers, and reports.\n2. **Human Agents**:\n   - Handle transferred calls and close deals.\n3. **IT Team**:\n   - Provide technical support and ongoing system maintenance.\n4. **Sponsor**:\n   - Marketing VP oversees funding and priority alignment.\n\n### External Users\n1. **Travel Agencies and Airlines**:\n   - Organizations using the system to connect with end customers.\n2. **Final Customers**:\n   - Recipients of the AI dialer calls.\n\n---\n\n## Assumptions & Constraints\n### Assumptions\n1. The marketing team will provide predefined offers and customer surveys to input in the database.\n2. The IT team has suitable resources for system deployment and maintenance.\n\n### Constraints\n1. ### Glossary for AI Dialer System

#### Business Terms:
- **CRM (Customer Relationship Management):** A system or software that manages a company's interactions with current and potential customers. In the context of AI Dialer, CRM integration allows for optimized customer outreach and data handling.
- **KPI (Key Performance Indicator):** Metrics used to evaluate the success of specific activities or overall performance. In the AI Dialer system, KPIs could include call success rates, conversion rates, and average call handling time.
- **GDPR Compliance (General Data Protection Regulation Compliance):** Adherence to data protection and privacy laws established by the European Union. For AI Dialers, this means safeguarding customer information during data storage, transmission, and processing.

#### Technical Terms:
- **SIP (Session Initiation Protocol):** A communication protocol used for initiating, maintaining, and terminating real-time sessions, like voice and video calls. Crucial for AI Dialers in interacting with VoIP systems.
- **Natural Language Processing (NLP):** A field of artificial intelligence focused on enabling computers to understand, interpret, and respond to human language. In AI Dialers, NLP is used for interpreting customer speech and generating appropriate responses.
- **RAG (Red-Amber-Green):** A status indicator framework frequently used in project management. For AI Dialers, it could represent live monitoring of system performance or issue resolution status.

#### System Features:
- **Predefined Responses:** A library of canned or templated answers programmed into the AI Dialer for common queries or circumstances, ensuring quick and consistent communication.
- **Navigation:** Tools and features within the AI Dialer interface that assist users in moving through the system efficiently.
- **Multilingual Capabilities:** The ability of the AI system to communicate and interpret languages other than English, broadening regional or global usage.

#### General Terms:
- **Communication Protocols:** Standards governing the exchange of information between systems and devices. In AI Dialer software, robust protocols ensure reliable and flexible communication processes.
- **Interoperability:** The capability of the AI Dialer system to integrate with other software or systems (e.g., CRM, VoIP platforms) seamlessly.

The AI will handle call interactions, transfer customers to human agents for finalizing deals, and aim to improve efficiency and customer engagement. By leveraging integrations with existing systems, the AI dialer will streamline outbound campaigns and provide comprehensive reporting on KPIs.\\n\\n---\\n\\n## Scope\\n### In-Scope Features\\n1. Integration with the call center to:\\n   - Initiate and transfer calls.\\n   - Support up to 300 simultaneous calls.\\n2. Integration with CRM systems for accessing the following customer details:\\n   - Name, phone number, location, and salary.\\n3. Database usage for:\\n   - Predefined offers and responses.\\n   - Storing call results (successful, rejected, or error calls).\\n   - Storing additional resources for generating responses to customer questions using a Rules and Guidance System (RAG).\\n4. Generation of end-of-shift metrics and KPIs, which include:\\n   - Successful calls, errors, successful deals, and rejected deals.\\n   - Reports featuring charts, tables, and a dynamic dashboard.\\n5. Real-time decision-making based on predefined offers and call status.\\n\\n### Out-of-Scope Features\\n1. Integration with WhatsApp or other social networks.\\n2. Expansion to non-English languages.\\n\\n---\\n\\n## Functional Requirements\\n1. The AI dialer must integrate with the call center system to initiate, manage, and transfer calls in real time.\\n2. The AI must access relevant customer data from the CRM system during the call execution process.\\n3. The system must store and retrieve predefined offers and responses from a database.\\n4. The AI should generate reports at the end of each shift, summarizing KPIs and metrics.\\n5. The system should allow marketing teams to update offers and predefined responses in the database.\\n6. Calls should operate only within the hours of 8:00 AM to 5:00 PM.\\n7. All data, including call results and customer interactions, must be stored securely with encryption.\\n\\n---\\n\\n## Non-Functional Requirements\\n1. **Performance**:\\n   - Support up to 300 simultaneous calls.\\n2. **Availability and Reliability**:\\n   - Ensure a system availability of 99.96%.\\n3. **Language & Voice Quality**:\\n   - Support only the English language.\\n   - Use natural language processing to achieve human-like voice quality with respectful and convincing tones.\\n4. **Security**:\\n   - Comply with GDPR and PII regulations.\\n   - Implement access controls: developers have full access, while the marketing team has restricted access to reports.\\n5. **Usability**:\\n   - Follow best practices for UI/UX design.\\n\\n---\\n\\n## User Roles & Personas\\n\\n### Internal Users\\n1. **Marketing Team**:\\n   - Updates predefined offers, answers, and reports.\\n2. **Human Agents**:\\n   - Handle transferred calls and close deals.\\n3. **IT Team**:\\n   - Provide technical support and ongoing system maintenance.\\n4. **Sponsor**:\\n   - Marketing VP oversees funding and priority alignment.\\n\\n### External Users\\n1. **Travel Agencies and Airlines**:\\n   - Organizations using the system to connect with end customers.\\n2. **Final Customers**:\\n   - Recipients of the AI dialer calls.\\n\\n---\\n\\n## Assumptions & Constraints\\n### Assumptions\\n1. The marketing team will provide predefined offers and customer surveys to input in the database.\\n2. The IT team has suitable resources for system deployment and maintenance.\\n\\n### Constraints\\n1. Calls will be restricted to working hours (8:00 AM–5:00 PM).\\n2. The system must comply with GDPR and PII regulations. \\n3. All offers and answers are predefined and stored in the database.  \\n\\n---\\n\\n## Use Cases\\n### **Use Case 1: Make an Outbound Call**\\n#### Description:\\nThe AI Dialer enables human agents to make outbound calls efficiently to customers, providing behavioral insights to tailor the call experience.\\n\\n#### Actors:\\n- Human Agents\\n- Customers\\n\\n#### Preconditions:\\n1. The agent must be logged into the AI Dialer system.\\n2. The customer’s contact information must exist in the database.\\n3. The outbound call feature must be enabled for the agent.\\n\\n#### Postconditions:\\n1. The customer receives the call.\\n2. The system records call details such as duration, outcome, and notes.\\n\\n#### Main Flow:\\n1. The agent initiates the outbound call through the AI Dialer system.\\n2. The AI Dialer retrieves the customer\'s contact information from the database.\\n3. The system analyzes the mood and behavior of the customer using past interactions or real-time data.\\n4. The dialer connects the call to the customer.\\n5. The agent communicates with the customer.\\n6. At the end of the call, the system prompts the agent to record notes and the call outcome.\\n7. If applicable, the system suggests follow-up actions based on the call\'s result.\\n\\n#### Alternate Flows:\\n1. **Call Fails to Connect:**\\n   1.1 The system retries the call three times before marking it as \\"Failed to Connect.\\"\\n   1.2 A notification is sent to the agent regarding failure.\\n \\n2. **Customer Busy:**\\n   2.1 The system schedules a callback and notifies the agent.\\n\\n---\\n\\n### **Use Case 2: Generate Call Reports**\\n#### Description:\\nThe AI Dialer provides detailed call reports for the marketing team and IT support to analyze performance and troubleshoot issues.\\n\\n#### Actors:\\n- Marketing Teams\\n- IT Support\\n\\n#### Preconditions:\\n1. Reports must have data from completed calls.\\n2. The user must have access permissions to view reports.\\n\\n#### Postconditions:\\n1. A summarized report is generated with metrics like call duration, success rate, and agent performance.\\n2. Users can download or share the report for further analysis.\\n\\n#### Main Flow:\\n1. The user logs into the AI Dialer system.\\n2. The user navigates to the reporting module.\\n3. The user requests a custom report by selecting filters such as date range, agent ID, or call outcomes.\\n4. The system processes and generates the report.\\n5. The user views the report on the screen and downloads it if necessary.\\n\\n#### Alternate Flows:\\n1. **Missing Data:**\\n   1.1 The system alerts the user about incomplete or missing records relevant to the report.\\n   1.2 The user can adjust the filters or wait for the data to refresh.\\n\\n2. **Export Error:**\\n   2.1 The system retries the export process automatically.\\n   2.2 If unsuccessful, the system prompts the user to make manual corrections.\\n\\n---\\n\\n## Glossary\\n### Business Terms\\n- **CRM (Customer Relationship Management):** A system or software that manages a company\'s interactions with current and potential customers. All data, including call results and customer interactions, must be stored securely with encryption.\\n\\n---\\n\\n## Non-Functional Requirements\\n1. **Performance**:\\n   - Support up to 300 simultaneous calls.\\n2. **Availability and Reliability**:\\n   - Ensure a system availability of 99.96%.\\n3. **Language & Voice Quality**:\\n   - Support only the English language.\\n   - Use natural language processing to achieve human-like voice quality with respectful and convincing tones.\\n4. **Security**:\\n   - Comply with GDPR and PII regulations.\\n   - Implement access controls: developers have full access, while the marketing team has restricted access to reports.\\n5. **Usability**:\\n   - Follow best practices for UI/UX design.\\n\\n---\\n\\n## User Roles & Personas\\n\\n### Internal Users\\n1. **Marketing Team**:\\n   - Updates predefined offers, answers, and reports.\\n2. **Human Agents**:\\n   - Handle transferred calls and close deals.\\n3. **IT Team**:\\n   - Provide technical support and ongoing system maintenance.\\n4. **Sponsor**:\\n   - Marketing VP oversees funding and priority alignment.\\n\\n### External Users\\n1. **Travel Agencies and Airlines**:\\n   - Organizations using the system to connect with end customers.\\n2. **Final Customers**:\\n   - Recipients of the AI dialer calls.\\n\\n---\\n\\n## Assumptions & Constraints\\n### Assumptions\\n1. The marketing team will provide predefined offers and customer surveys to input in the database.\\n2. The IT team has suitable resources for system deployment and maintenance.\\n\\n### Constraints\\n1. Calls will be restricted to working hours (8:00 AM–5:00 PM).\\n2. The system must comply with GDPR and PII regulations. \\n3. All offers and answers are predefined and stored in the database.  \\n\\n---\\n\\n## Use Cases\\n### **Use Case 1: Make an Outbound Call**\\n#### Description:\\nThe AI Dialer enables human agents to make outbound calls efficiently to customers, providing behavioral insights to tailor the call experience.\\n\\n#### Actors:\\n- Human Agents\\n- Customers\\n\\n#### Preconditions:\\n1. The agent must be logged into the AI Dialer system.\\n2. The customer’s contact information must exist in the database.\\n3. The outbound call feature must be enabled for the agent.\\n\\n#### Postconditions:\\n1. The customer receives the call.\\n2. The system records call details such as duration, outcome, and notes.\\n\\n#### Main Flow:\\n1. The agent initiates the outbound call through the AI Dialer system.\\n2. The AI Dialer retrieves the customer\'s contact information from the database.\\n3. The system analyzes the mood and behavior of the customer using past interactions or real-time data.\\n4. The dialer connects the call to the customer.\\n5. The agent communicates with the customer.\\n6. At the end of the call, the system prompts the agent to record notes and the call outcome.\\n7. If applicable, the system suggests follow-up actions based on the call\'s result.\\n\\n#### Alternate Flows:\\n1. **Call Fails to Connect:**\\n   1.1 The system retries the call three times before marking it as \\"Failed to Connect.\\"\\n   1.2 A notification is sent to the agent regarding failure.\\n \\n2. **Customer Busy:**\\n   2.1 The system schedules a callback and notifies the agent.\\n\\n---\\n\\n### **Use Case 2: Generate Call Reports**\\n#### Description:\\nThe AI Dialer provides detailed call reports for the marketing team and IT support to analyze performance and troubleshoot issues.\\n\\n#### Actors:\\n- Marketing Teams\\n- IT Support\\n\\n#### Preconditions:\\n1. Reports must have data from completed calls.\\n2. The user must have access permissions to view reports.\\n\\n#### Postconditions:\\n1. A summarized report is generated with metrics like call duration, success rate, and agent performance.\\n2. Users can download or share the report for further analysis.\\n\\n#### Main Flow:\\n1. The user logs into the AI Dialer system.\\n2. The user navigates to the reporting module.\\n3. The user requests a custom report by selecting filters such as date range, agent ID, or call outcomes.\\n4. The system processes and generates the report.\\n5. The user views the report on the screen and downloads it if necessary.\\n\\n#### Alternate Flows:\\n1. **Missing Data:**\\n   1.1 The system alerts the user about incomplete or missing records relevant to the report.\\n   1.2 The user can adjust the filters or wait for the data to refresh.\\n\\n2. **Export Error:**\\n   2.1 The system retries the export process automatically.\\n   2.2 If unsuccessful, the system prompts the user to make manual corrections.\\n\\n---\\n\\n## Glossary\\n### Business Terms\\n- **CRM (Customer Relationship Management):** A system or software that manages a company\'s interactions with current and potential customers. In the context of AI Dialer, CRM integration allows for optimized customer outreach and data handling.\\n- **KPI (Key Performance Indicator):** Metrics used to evaluate the success of specific activities or overall performance. In the AI Dialer system, KPIs could include call success rates, conversion rates, and average call handling time.\\n- **GDPR Compliance (General Data Protection Regulation Compliance):** Adherence to data protection and privacy laws established by the European Union. For AI Dialers, this means safeguarding customer information during data storage, transmission, and processing.\\n\\n### Technical Terms\\n- **SIP (Session Initiation Protocol):** A communication protocol used for initiating, maintaining, and terminating real-time sessions, like voice and video calls. Crucial for AI Dialers in interacting with VoIP systems.\\n- **Natural Language Processing (NLP):** A field of artificial intelligence focused on enabling computers to understand, interpret, and respond to human language. In AI Dialers, NLP is used for interpreting customer speech and generating appropriate responses.\\n- **RAG (Red-Amber-Green):** A status indicator framework frequently used in project management. For AI Dialers, it could represent live monitoring of system performance or issue resolution status.\\n\\n### System Features\\n- **Predefined Responses:** A library of canned or templated answers programmed into the AI Dialer for common queries or circumstances, ensuring quick and consistent communication.\\n- **Navigation:** Tools and features within the AI Dialer interface that assist users in moving through the system efficiently.\\n\\n### General Terms\\n- **Communication Protocols:** Standards governing the exchange of information between systems and devices. In AI Dialer software, robust protocols ensure reliable and flexible communication processes.\\n- **Interoperability:** The capability of the AI Dialer system to integrate with other software or systems (e.g., CRM, VoIP platforms) seamlessly.","file_path":"../conversation/functional_specification.md"}', name='write_md_file_tool')] type='ToolCallRequestEvent'

id='6d70c49c-e18e-4510-972d-995975460ee5' source='functional_spec_checker_agent' models_usage=None metadata={} created_at=datetime.datetime(2025, 8, 9, 20, 56, 55, 653029, tzinfo=datetime.timezone.utc) content=[FunctionExecutionResult(content='# Functional Specification Document\n\n## Title Page\n**Project Name**: AI Dialer for Travel Packages\n\n**Version**: 1.0\n\n**Date**: 2023-11-03\n\n---\n\n## Introduction\nThe purpose of this project is to develop an AI-powered dialer system designed to call customers and offer travel packages. The AI will handle call interactions, transfer customers to human agents for finalizing deals, and aim to improve efficiency and customer engagement. By leveraging integrations with existing systems, the AI dialer will streamline outbound campaigns and provide comprehensive reporting on KPIs.\n\n---\n\n## Scope\n### In-Scope Features\n1. Integration with the call center to:\n   - Initiate and transfer calls.\n   - Support up to 300 simultaneous calls.\n2. Integration with CRM systems for accessing the following customer details:\n   - Name, phone number, location, and salary.\n3. Database usage for:\n   - Predefined offers and responses.\n   - Storing call results (successful, rejected, or error calls).\n   - Storing additional resources for generating responses to customer questions using a Rules and Guidance System (RAG).\n4. Generation of end-of-shift metrics and KPIs, which include:\n   - Successful calls, errors, successful deals, and rejected deals.\n   - Reports featuring charts, tables, and a dynamic dashboard.\n5. Real-time decision-making based on predefined offers and call status.\n\n### Out-of-Scope Features\n1. Integration with WhatsApp or other social networks.\n2. Expansion to non-English languages.\n\n---\n\n## Functional Requirements\n1. The AI dialer must integrate with the call center system to initiate, manage, and transfer calls in real time.\n2. The AI must access relevant customer data from the CRM system during the call execution process.\n3. The system must store and retrieve predefined offers and responses from a database.\n4. The AI should generate reports at the end of each shift, summarizing KPIs and metrics.\n5. The system should allow marketing teams to update offers and predefined responses in the database.\n6. Calls should operate only within the hours of 8:00 AM to 5:00 PM.\n7. All data, including call results and customer interactions, must be stored securely with encryption.\n\n---\n\n## Non-Functional Requirements\n1. **Performance**:\n   - Support up to 300 simultaneous calls.\n2. **Availability and Reliability**:\n   - Ensure a system availability of 99.96%.\n3. **Language & Voice Quality**:\n   - Support only the English language.\n   - Use natural language processing to achieve human-like voice quality with respectful and convincing tones.\n4. **Security**:\n   - Comply with GDPR and PII regulations.\n   - Implement access controls: developers have full access, while the marketing team has restricted access to reports.\n5. **Usability**:\n   - Follow best practices for UI/UX design.\n\n---\n\n## User Roles & Personas\n\n### Internal Users\n1. **Marketing Team**:\n   - Updates predefined offers, answers, and reports.\n2. **Human Agents**:\n   - Handle transferred calls and close deals.\n3. **IT Team**:\n   - Provide technical support and ongoing system maintenance.\n4. **Sponsor**:\n   - Marketing VP oversees funding and priority alignment.\n\n### External Users\n1. **Travel Agencies and Airlines**:\n   - Organizations using the system to connect with end customers.\n2. **Final Customers**:\n   - Recipients of the AI dialer calls.\n\n---\n\n## Assumptions & Constraints\n### Assumptions\n1. The marketing team will provide predefined offers and customer surveys to input in the database.\n2. The IT team has suitable resources for system deployment and maintenance.\n\n### Constraints\n1. Calls will be restricted to working hours (8:00 AM–5:00 PM).\n2. The system must comply with GDPR and PII regulations. \n3. All offers and answers are predefined and stored in the database.  \n\n---\n\n## Use Cases\n### **Use Case 1: Make an Outbound Call**\n#### Description:\nThe AI Dialer enables human agents to make outbound calls efficiently to customers, providing behavioral insights to tailor the call experience.\n\n#### Actors:\n- Human Agents\n- Customers\n\n#### Preconditions:\n1. The agent must be logged into the AI Dialer system.\n2. The customer’s contact information must exist in the database.\n3. The outbound call feature must be enabled for the agent.\n\n#### Postconditions:\n1. The customer receives the call.\n2. The system records call details such as duration, outcome, and notes.\n\n#### Main Flow:\n1. The agent initiates the outbound call through the AI Dialer system.\n2. The AI Dialer retrieves the customer\'s contact information from the database.\n3. The system analyzes the mood and behavior of the customer using past interactions or real-time data.\n4. The dialer connects the call to the customer.\n5. The agent communicates with the customer.\n6. At the end of the call, the system prompts the agent to record notes and the call outcome.\n7. If applicable, the system suggests follow-up actions based on the call\'s result.\n\n#### Alternate Flows:\n1. **Call Fails to Connect:**\n   1.1 The system retries the call three times before marking it as "Failed to Connect."\n   1.2 A notification is sent to the agent regarding failure.\n \n2. **Customer Busy:**\n   2.1 The system schedules a callback and notifies the agent.\n\n---\n\n### **Use Case 2: Generate Call Reports**\n#### Description:\nThe AI Dialer provides detailed call reports for the marketing team and IT support to analyze performance and troubleshoot issues.\n\n#### Actors:\n- Marketing Teams\n- IT Support\n\n#### Preconditions:\n1. Reports must have data from completed calls.\n2. The user must have access permissions to view reports.\n\n#### Postconditions:\n1. A summarized report is generated with metrics like call duration, success rate, and agent performance.\n2. Users can download or share the report for further analysis.\n\n#### Main Flow:\n1. The user logs into the AI Dialer system.\n2. The user navigates to the reporting module.\n3. The user requests a custom report by selecting filters such as date range, agent ID, or call outcomes.\n4. The system processes and generates the report.\n5. The user views the report on the screen and downloads it if necessary.\n\n#### Alternate Flows:\n1. **Missing Data:**\n   1.1 The system alerts the user about incomplete or missing records relevant to the report.\n   1.2 The user can adjust the filters or wait for the data to refresh.\n\n2. **Export Error:**\n   2.1 The system retries the export process automatically.\n   2.2 If unsuccessful, the system prompts the user to make manual corrections.\n\n---\n\n## Glossary\n### Business Terms\n- **CRM (Customer Relationship Management):** A system or software that manages a company\'s interactions with current and potential customers. In the context of AI Dialer, CRM integration allows for optimized customer outreach and data handling.\n- **KPI (Key Performance Indicator):** Metrics used to evaluate the success of specific activities or overall performance. In the AI Dialer system, KPIs could include call success rates, conversion rates, and average call handling time.\n- **GDPR Compliance (General Data Protection Regulation Compliance):** Adherence to data protection and privacy laws established by the European Union. For AI Dialers, this means safeguarding customer information during data storage, transmission, and processing.\n\n### Technical Terms\n- **SIP (Session Initiation Protocol):** A communication protocol used for initiating, maintaining, and terminating real-time sessions, like voice and video calls. Crucial for AI Dialers in interacting with VoIP systems.\n- **Natural Language Processing (NLP):** A field of artificial intelligence focused on enabling computers to understand, interpret, and respond to human language. In AI Dialers, NLP is used for interpreting customer speech and generating appropriate responses.\n- **RAG (Red-Amber-Green):** A status indicator framework frequently used in project management. For AI Dialers, it could represent live monitoring of system performance or issue resolution status.\n\n### System Features\n- **Predefined Responses:** A library of canned or templated answers programmed into the AI Dialer for common queries or circumstances, ensuring quick and consistent communication.\n- **Navigation:** Tools and features within the AI Dialer interface that assist users in moving through the system efficiently.\n\n### General Terms\n- **Communication Protocols:** Standards governing the exchange of information between systems and devices. In AI Dialer software, robust protocols ensure reliable and flexible communication processes.\n- **Interoperability:** The capability of the AI Dialer system to integrate with other software or systems (e.g., CRM, VoIP platforms) seamlessly.', name='read_md_file_tool', call_id='call_gJ5ISLGjDYeQh4uXQdRP2TQZ', is_error=False)] type='ToolCallExecutionEvent'

Integration with CRM systems for accessing the following customer details:\n   - Name, phone number, location, and salary.\n3. Database usage for:\n   - Predefined offers and responses.\n   - Storing call results (successful, rejected, or error calls).\n   - Storing additional resources for generating responses to customer questions using a Rules and Guidance System (RAG).\n4. Generation of end-of-shift metrics and KPIs, which include:\n   - Successful calls, errors, successful deals, and rejected deals.\n   - Reports featuring charts, tables, and a dynamic dashboard.\n5. Real-time decision-making based on predefined offers and call status.\n\n### Out-of-Scope Features\n1. Integration with WhatsApp or other social networks.\n2. Expansion to non-English languages.\n\n---\n\n## Functional Requirements\n1. The AI dialer must integrate with the call center system to initiate, manage, and transfer calls in real time.\n2. The AI must access relevant customer data from the CRM system during the call execution process.\n3. The system must store and retrieve predefined offers and responses from a database.\n4. The AI should generate reports at the end of each shift, summarizing KPIs and metrics.\n5. The system should allow marketing teams to update offers and predefined responses in the database.\n6. Calls should operate only within the hours of 8:00 AM to 5:00 PM.\n7. All data, including call results and customer interactions, must be stored securely with encryption.\n\n---\n\n## Non-Functional Requirements\n1. **Performance**:\n   - Support up to 300 simultaneous calls.\n2. **Availability and Reliability**:\n   - Ensure a system availability of 99.96%.\n3. **Language & Voice Quality**:\n   - Support only the English language.\n   - Use natural language processing to achieve human-like voice quality with respectful and convincing tones.\n4. **Security**:\n   - Comply with GDPR and PII regulations.\n   - Implement access controls: developers have full access, while the marketing team has restricted access to reports.\n5. **Usability**:\n   - Follow best practices for UI/UX design.\n\n---\n\n## User Roles & Personas\n\n### Internal Users\n1. **Marketing Team**:\n   - Updates predefined offers, answers, and reports.\n2. **Human Agents**:\n   - Handle transferred calls and close deals.\n3. **IT Team**:\n   - Provide technical support and ongoing system maintenance.\n4. **Sponsor**:\n   - Marketing VP oversees funding and priority alignment.\n\n### External Users\n1. **Travel Agencies and Airlines**:\n   - Organizations using the system to connect with end customers.\n2. **Final Customers**:\n   - Recipients of the AI dialer calls.\n\n---\n\n## Assumptions & Constraints\n### Assumptions\n1. The marketing team will provide predefined offers and customer surveys to input in the database.\n2. The IT team has suitable resources for system deployment and maintenance.\n\n### Constraints\n1. Calls will be restricted to working hours (8:00 AM–5:00 PM).\n2. The system must comply with GDPR and PII regulations. \n3. All offers and answers are predefined and stored in the database.  \n\n---\n\n## Use Cases\n### **Use Case 1: Make an Outbound Call**\n#### Description:\nThe AI Dialer enables human agents to make outbound calls efficiently to customers, providing behavioral insights to tailor the call experience.\n\n#### Actors:\n- Human Agents\n- Customers\n\n#### Preconditions:\n1. The agent must be logged into the AI Dialer system.\n2. The customer’s contact information must exist in the database.\n3. The outbound call feature must be enabled for the agent.\n\n#### Postconditions:\n1. The customer receives the call.\n2. The system records call details such as duration, outcome, and notes.\n\n#### Main Flow:\n1. The agent initiates the outbound call through the AI Dialer system.\n2. The AI Dialer retrieves the customer\'s contact information from the database.\n3. The system analyzes the mood and behavior of the customer using past interactions or real-time data.\n4. The dialer connects the call to the customer.\n5. The agent communicates with the customer.\n6. At the end of the call, the system prompts the agent to record notes and the call outcome.\n7. If applicable, the system suggests follow-up actions based on the call\'s result.\n\n#### Alternate Flows:\n1. **Call Fails to Connect:**\n   1.1 The system retries the call three times before marking it as "Failed to Connect."\n   1.2 A notification is sent to the agent regarding failure.\n \n2. **Customer Busy:**\n   2.1 The system schedules a callback and notifies the agent.\n\n---\n\n### **Use Case 2: Generate Call Reports**\n#### Description:\nThe AI Dialer provides detailed call reports for the marketing team and IT support to analyze performance and troubleshoot issues.\n\n#### Actors:\n- Marketing Teams\n- IT Support\n\n#### Preconditions:\n1. Reports must have data from completed calls.\n2. The user must have access permissions to view reports.\n\n#### Postconditions:\n1. A summarized report is generated with metrics like call duration, success rate, and agent performance.\n2. Users can download or share the report for further analysis.\n\n#### Main Flow:\n1. The user logs into the AI Dialer system.\n2. The user navigates to the reporting module.\n3. The user requests a custom report by selecting filters such as date range, agent ID, or call outcomes.\n4. The system processes and generates the report.\n5. The user views the report on the screen and downloads it if necessary.\n\n#### Alternate Flows:\n1. **Missing Data:**\n   1.1 The system alerts the user about incomplete or missing records relevant to the report.\n   1.2 The user can adjust the filters or wait for the data to refresh.\n\n2. **Export Error:**\n   2.1 The system retries the export process automatically.\n   2.2 If unsuccessful, the system prompts the user to make manual corrections.\n\n---\n\n## Glossary\n### Business Terms\n- **CRM (Customer Relationship Management):** A system or software that manages a company\'s interactions with current and potential customers. In the context of AI Dialer, CRM integration allows for optimized customer outreach and data handling.\n- **KPI (Key Performance Indicator):** Metrics used to evaluate the success of specific activities or overall performance. In the AI Dialer system, KPIs could include call success rates, conversion rates, and average call handling time.\n- **GDPR Compliance (General Data Protection Regulation Compliance):** Adherence to data protection and privacy laws established by the European Union. For AI Dialers, this means safeguarding customer information during data storage, transmission, and processing.\n\n### Technical Terms\n- **SIP (Session Initiation Protocol):** A communication protocol used for initiating, maintaining, and terminating real-time sessions, like voice and video calls. Crucial for AI Dialers in interacting with VoIP systems.\n- **Natural Language Processing (NLP):** A field of artificial intelligence focused on enabling computers to understand, interpret, and respond to human language. In AI Dialers, NLP is used for interpreting customer speech and generating appropriate responses.\n- **RAG (Red-Amber-Green):** A status indicator framework frequently used in project management. For AI Dialers, it could represent live monitoring of system performance or issue resolution status.\n\n### System Features\n- **Predefined Responses:** A library of canned or templated answers programmed into the AI Dialer for common queries or circumstances, ensuring quick and consistent communication.\n- **Navigation:** Tools and features within the AI Dialer interface that assist users in moving through the system efficiently.\n\n### General Terms\n- **Communication Protocols:** Standards governing the exchange of information between systems and devices. In AI Dialer software, robust protocols ensure reliable and flexible communication processes.\n- **Interoperability:** The capability of the AI Dialer system to integrate with other software or systems (e.g., CRM, VoIP platforms) seamlessly.', name='read_md_file_tool', call_id='call_gJ5ISLGjDYeQh4uXQdRP2TQZ', is_error=False)]

The AI must access relevant customer data from the CRM system during the call execution process.\n3. The system must store and retrieve predefined offers and responses from a database.\n4. The AI should generate reports at the end of each shift, summarizing KPIs and metrics.\n5. The system should allow marketing teams to update offers and predefined responses in the database.\n6. Calls should operate only within the hours of 8:00 AM to 5:00 PM.\n7. All data, including call results and customer interactions, must be stored securely with encryption.\n\n---\n\n## Non-Functional Requirements\n1. **Performance**:\n   - Support up to 300 simultaneous calls.\n2. **Availability and Reliability**:\n   - Ensure a system availability of 99.96%.\n3. **Language & Voice Quality**:\n   - Support only the English language.\n   - Use natural language processing to achieve human-like voice quality with respectful and convincing tones.\n4. **Security**:\n   - Comply with GDPR and PII regulations.\n   - Implement access controls: developers have full access, while the marketing team has restricted access to reports.\n5. **Usability**:\n   - Follow best practices for UI/UX design.\n\n---\n\n## User Roles & Personas\n\n### Internal Users\n1. **Marketing Team**:\n   - Updates predefined offers, answers, and reports.\n2. **Human Agents**:\n   - Handle transferred calls and close deals.\n3. **IT Team**:\n   - Provide technical support and ongoing system maintenance.\n4. **Sponsor**:\n   - Marketing VP oversees funding and priority alignment.\n\n### External Users\n1. **Travel Agencies and Airlines**:\n   - Organizations using the system to connect with end customers.\n2. **Final Customers**:\n   - Recipients of the AI dialer calls.\n\n---\n\n## Assumptions & Constraints\n### Assumptions\n1. The marketing team will provide predefined offers and customer surveys to input in the database.\n2. The IT team has suitable resources for system deployment and maintenance.\n\n### Constraints\n1. Calls will be restricted to working hours (8:00 AM–5:00 PM).\n2. The system must comply with GDPR and PII regulations. \n3. All offers and answers are predefined and stored in the database.  \n\n---\n\n## Use Cases\n### **Use Case 1: Make an Outbound Call**\n#### Description:\nThe AI Dialer enables human agents to make outbound calls efficiently to customers, providing behavioral insights to tailor the call experience.\n\n#### Actors:\n- Human Agents\n- Customers\n\n#### Preconditions:\n1. The agent must be logged into the AI Dialer system.\n2. The customer’s contact information must exist in the database.\n3. The outbound call feature must be enabled for the agent.\n\n#### Postconditions:\n1. The customer receives the call.\n2. The system records call details such as duration, outcome, and notes.\n\n#### Main Flow:\n1. The agent initiates the outbound call through the AI Dialer system.\n2. The AI Dialer retrieves the customer\'s contact information from the database.\n3. The system analyzes the mood and behavior of the customer using past interactions or real-time data.\n4. The dialer connects the call to the customer.\n5. The agent communicates with the customer.\n6. At the end of the call, the system prompts the agent to record notes and the call outcome.\n7. If applicable, the system suggests follow-up actions based on the call\'s result.\n\n#### Alternate Flows:\n1. **Call Fails to Connect:**\n   1.1 The system retries the call three times before marking it as "Failed to Connect."\n   1.2 A notification is sent to the agent regarding failure.\n \n2. **Customer Busy:**\n   2.1 The system schedules a callback and notifies the agent.\n\n---\n\n### **Use Case 2: Generate Call Reports**\n#### Description:\nThe AI Dialer provides detailed call reports for the marketing team and IT support to analyze performance and troubleshoot issues.\n\n#### Actors:\n- Marketing Teams\n- IT Support\n\n#### Preconditions:\n1. Reports must have data from completed calls.\n2. The user must have access permissions to view reports.\n\n#### Postconditions:\n1. A summarized report is generated with metrics like call duration, success rate, and agent performance.\n2. Users can download or share the report for further analysis.\n\n#### Main Flow:\n1. The user logs into the AI Dialer system.\n2. The user navigates to the reporting module.\n3. The user requests a custom report by selecting filters such as date range, agent ID, or call outcomes.\n4. The system processes and generates the report.\n5. The user views the report on the screen and downloads it if necessary.\n\n#### Alternate Flows:\n1. **Missing Data:**\n   1.1 The system alerts the user about incomplete or missing records relevant to the report.\n   1.2 The user can adjust the filters or wait for the data to refresh.\n\n2. **Export Error:**\n   2.1 The system retries the export process automatically.\n   2.2 If unsuccessful, the system prompts the user to make manual corrections.\n\n---\n\n## Glossary\n### Business Terms\n- **CRM (Customer Relationship Management):** A system or software that manages a company\'s interactions with current and potential customers. In the context of AI Dialer, CRM integration allows for optimized customer outreach and data handling.\n- **KPI (Key Performance Indicator):** Metrics used to evaluate the success of specific activities or overall performance. In the AI Dialer system, KPIs could include call success rates, conversion rates, and average call handling time.\n- **GDPR Compliance (General Data Protection Regulation Compliance):** Adherence to data protection and privacy laws established by the European Union. For AI Dialers, this means safeguarding customer information during data storage, transmission, and processing.\n\n### Technical Terms\n- **SIP (Session Initiation Protocol):** A communication protocol used for initiating, maintaining, and terminating real-time sessions, like voice and video calls. Crucial for AI Dialers in interacting with VoIP systems.\n- **Natural Language Processing (NLP):** A field of artificial intelligence focused on enabling computers to understand, interpret, and respond to human language. In AI Dialers, NLP is used for interpreting customer speech and generating appropriate responses.\n- **RAG (Red-Amber-Green):** A status indicator framework frequently used in project management. For AI Dialers, it could represent live monitoring of system performance or issue resolution status.\n\n### System Features\n- **Predefined Responses:** A library of canned or templated answers programmed into the AI Dialer for common queries or circumstances, ensuring quick and consistent communication.\n- **Navigation:** Tools and features within the AI Dialer interface that assist users in moving through the system efficiently.\n\n### General Terms\n- **Communication Protocols:** Standards governing the exchange of information between systems and devices. In AI Dialer software, robust protocols ensure reliable and flexible communication processes.\n- **Interoperability:** The capability of the AI Dialer system to integrate with other software or systems (e.g., CRM, VoIP platforms) seamlessly.', name='read_md_file_tool', call_id='call_iLnVs1DXFwaoVLRvbtzGIuWL', is_error=False)] type='ToolCallExecutionEvent'
